- **docs-update-with-pr**: PRs that touch `rytm_randomizer/` or `pyproject.toml` MUST also touch at least one of `docs/`, `README.md`, or `CONTRIBUTING.md`. This is enforced by the CI `docs-gate` job. Use this skill before opening a PR that modifies code or dependencies, when CI fails with a "docs-gate" failure, or when a contributor asks "do I really need to update docs for a pure refactor?" The answer is yes — even a one-line `docs/STATUS.md` bump counts. Docs rot is the dominant onboarding problem in this repo; tying doc touches to code changes is how we prevent drift.
